visible_text_exited: "Daytona: Spot GPU sandboxes are now live for everyone! Run GPU workloads on preemptible capacity at a fraction of on-demand pricing: • No quotas: spot GPUs don't count against your GPU quota. Available capacity is the only limit. . • Deeply discounted: significantly cheaper than on-demand GPUs, see the pricing page: [ https://www.daytona.io/pricing (link)] . • All GPU types: H100, H200, RTX Pro 6000, RTX 4090, RTX 5090, with ordered fallback between types. . Getting started: choose a GPU, set resources, and use any Docker image: sandbox = daytona.create( CreateSandboxFromImageParams( image=\"nvidia/cuda:12.4.1-base-ubuntu22.04\", auto_delete_interval=0, spot=True, resources=Resources(gpu=1, gpu_type=GpuType.H100), ) ) Heads up: spot sandboxes are preemptible. They can be reclaimed at any time when on-demand capacity is needed, so design workloads to tolerate interruption. Preempted sandboxes get a spotEvictedAt timestamp and stay retrievable for 24 hours. Try it: [ https://app.daytona.io/ (link)] Docs: [ https://www.daytona.io/docs/sandboxes#spot-gpu-sandboxes (link)] Questions? Drop them here or at [ support@daytona.io (link)] saluting face emoji. 8:38 AM. 4 links."
